- **binner-performance-comparison**: Use when reproducing or extending binner benchmark comparisons on simulated metagenomic datasets (e.g., CAMI II short- or long-read) where per-environment high-quality bin counts and AMBER precision/recall/F1 must be tabulated per tool. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/binner-performance-comparison))
- **binning-5percent**: 头尾5%分箱（Head-Tail 5% Binning）—— 首尾精细分箱，识别极端风险人群。完全复用 binning_v2 原始代码，不修改任何分箱逻辑，不暴露任何用户参数。 ([shihannn02/AIWeb](https://github.com/shihannn02/AIWeb/tree/HEAD/binning-5percent) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **binning-benchmark-percent-gain-computation**: Use when comparing binning strategies on long-read metagenomic datasets where the only varying factor is the clustering step (ensemble vs single-epsilon DBSCAN), and per-environment high-quality bin counts must be converted into percentage gains. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/binning-benchmark-percent-gain-computation))
- **binning-chisquare**: 卡方分箱（Chi-Square Binning）—— 用卡方合并算法对连续变量分箱，支持 OOT 切分 ([shihannn02/AIWeb](https://github.com/shihannn02/AIWeb/tree/HEAD/2026_05_31_binning-quantile/binning-chisquare) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **binning-output-quality-control**: Use when a bucket table produced by NMRProcFlow binning (Intelligent, variable-size or fixed-width) contains buckets with weak signal, and buckets must be retained or discarded by signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) before downstream statistics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binning-output-quality-control))
- **binning-quantile**: 等频分箱（Quantile Binning）—— 用 pd.qcut 对连续变量做等频分箱，支持 OOT 切分 ([shihannn02/AIWeb](https://github.com/shihannn02/AIWeb/tree/HEAD/2026_05_31_binning-quantile/binning-quantile) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **binny**: Pointer skill for BINNY, OpenBin's open-source agent harness for reverse engineering and malware analysis (Ghidra decompilation, sandboxed scripts, STIX triage, report drafts). Use when the user mentions BINNY or OpenBin, or wants a binary, APK or firmware analyzed with an agent. ([nuroctane/nur-cli](https://github.com/nuroctane/nur-cli/tree/HEAD/skills/binny) · ★5)
- **bino-authoring**: The disciplined loop for authoring bino report manifests (YAML + SQL) through the bino MCP server - outline the kind, scaffold the document, learn a dataset's columns, draft, validate_draft, write, then validate_project and build. Use whenever creating, editing, or wiring bino manifests (DataSource, DataSet, Table, Text, Tree, ChartTime, ChartStructure, LayoutPage, ReportArtefact, …) or fixing validation diagnostics. Assumes bino-concepts; pairs with bino-ibcs for the report semantics. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-authoring) · ★6)
- **bino-concepts**: bino's mental model in one read - the manifest envelope, the DataSource → DataSet → LayoutPage → artefact pipeline, why metadata.name is the SQL table name, dependencies, the standard dataset columns, ref vs inline children, LayoutPage params, constraints, inline datasets, ${VAR} substitution, bino.toml, and what validate_draft / validate_project / build each catch. Read before authoring any bino manifest, and whenever a diagnostic or a brief mentions dependencies, a scenario column, ref, params, a constraint, an inline dataset, an unresolved variable, a registry package, or bino.toml. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-concepts) · ★6)
- **bino-data-modeling**: Map raw source columns onto IBCS scenario slots (ac/pp/fc/pl), derive variances (d_/dr_), choose the grain, and write disciplined typed DataSet SQL for a bino report. Use when the bino-data autopilot phase models a source into datasets, or when co-authoring a DataSet. Owns the honest "the data can't satisfy this" - populate unmet[] rather than fabricate a column. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-data-modeling) · ★6)
- **bino-ibcs**: The IBCS rubric for bino reports - the semantics the schema can't carry. The SUCCESS rules (SAY · UNIFY · CONDENSE · CHECK · EXPRESS · SIMPLIFY · STRUCTURE), scenarios (ac/pp/fc/pl), variances (d_/dr_ … pos/neg/neu/op/opinv), analysis notations (_ ~ Ø ..), choosing between Table / ChartTime / ChartStructure / Tree, and data-aware narrative Text. Use when modeling a report's scenarios and variances, picking a visualization, writing commentary, or auditing IBCS compliance. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-ibcs) · ★6)
- **bino-orchestration**: The bino autopilot policy - the phase sequence, the autonomy tiers, the four checkpoint gates, the eight hard safety gates, the run-start preflight, the subagent-spawn contract, and the fix-loop policy. Used by /bino:autopilot on the main thread to sequence the bino subagents and hold the gates. The orchestrator never authors or edits manifests - it routes. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-orchestration) · ★6)
- **bino-packaging**: Author, check and publish a reusable bino registry package (a predef project) through the bino MCP server - scaffold it with init_bundle, fill the [package] table, name and parameterize its definitions, validate, run registry_publish_check, and hand the irreversible publish to the human. Use whenever the human wants to create a package, turn components into a package, release a new version of one, or asks why the registry rejected it. Consuming packages is in bino-authoring. ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-packaging) · ★6)
- **bino-requirements**: Elicit report requirements from the human and produce the REPORT BRIEF - the structured contract (audience, primary message, scenarios, variance, granularity, visualization intent, acceptance criteria) that the bino autopilot subagents consume. Use during /bino:autopilot phase 1 to turn a free-text goal into a brief. Runs on the main thread (it asks the human). ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-requirements) · ★6)
- **bino-validation-loop**: The four-layer VERDICT check (schema, IBCS, build-readiness, acceptance) and the bounded fix-loop policy for bino autopilot - diagnostic routing, the 2-iteration cap, and the stop-on-repeat rule. Used by the bino-validation subagent (to produce the VERDICT) and by the orchestrator (to drive the fix loop). Diagnose, never edit; PASS means "ready for sign-off," never "done." ([bino-bi/bino-cli](https://github.com/bino-bi/bino-cli/tree/HEAD/claude-plugin/skills/bino-validation-loop) · ★6)
- **binocular-vision**: Binocular Vision & Ocular Motility Evaluation: Cover-uncover and alternating cover tests, prism bar dissociation, near point of convergence (NPC), stereopsis (Titmus/Randot), and phoria vs tropia. ([Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills](https://github.com/Mr-Nobody-Anonymous/All-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome_skills/optometry/binocular-vision))
- **binome**: Acceptation en binôme avec Agent2Test, à la main (remise du plan validé et commité sur l'arbre de la demande propre, patchs remis présents, attente du journal de tests dont le développeur donne le chemin ou déclaration sans binôme, lecture par lire-acceptation.mjs, verdict par critère repris du script, défauts confirmés rouverts en cycle de correction dans leur budget, défauts contestés, non confirmés, régressions et journaux non valables présentés au développeur, rien d'écrit chez Agent2Test). À implémenter au lot 6. ([FredGarcia/Agent2Prompt](https://github.com/FredGarcia/Agent2Prompt/tree/HEAD/specs/agent2dev/modules/binome))
- **binome**: Skill Hephaistos normalisee binome — voir le SKILL.md canonique dans .agent/skills/binome pour la procedure complete. ([jeanCodeUnMax/work-space](https://github.com/jeanCodeUnMax/work-space/tree/HEAD/plugin-devin/skills/binome))
- **binome**: Protocole binôme maître souverain réservé exclusivement au tandem Tango (Antigravity) & Cash (Devin). Unifie et orchestre les compétences /chat-id et /jointes, assure la persistance de liaison post-redémarrage de l'IDE, la synchronisation bidirectionnelle et le commandement des agents subalternes. ([jeanCodeUnMax/work-space](https://github.com/jeanCodeUnMax/work-space/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/binome))
- **binome-agent2test**: Binôme avec Agent2Test, en local, enchaîné à la main par le développeur, ou par un échange par dossiers qui ne décide rien (rôles des deux têtes, étiquettes du binôme dans le fichier Gherkin, remise du plan validé tel quel et commité, campagne conduite par le développeur, lecture du journal de tests par le script lire-acceptation.mjs, verdict d'acceptation par critère sur le plan et le code courants, défaut applicatif confirmé corrigé en cycle TDD, causes qui ne sont pas des défauts, régression d'une autre demande, désaccord remonté au développeur, mode parallèle). À lire avant V2 pour les ét… ([FredGarcia/Agent2Prompt](https://github.com/FredGarcia/Agent2Prompt/tree/HEAD/specs/agent-tdd-jira/modules/binome-agent2test))
- **binomial**: Binomial Theorem quiz covering binomial expansions and coefficient extraction. Teaches students to: - Calculate nCr (n choose r) using the combination formula - Find coefficients in (1+x)^n expansions - Find coefficients in (a+bx)^n expansions using the binomial coefficient formula - Extract specific terms from binomial expansions ([vicharanashala/tenali](https://github.com/vicharanashala/tenali/tree/HEAD/supermarket/binomial) · ★20 · 3 copies)
- **binomial**: Interactive tutor for binomial option pricing. Walks a student through replicating portfolios and no-arbitrage pricing in a one-period tree, risk- neutral probabilities, two-period and n-period backward induction, and American options and early exercise — asking the student to work problems and giving diagnostic feedback on their answers. Use this whenever a student wants to learn, review, practice, or get unstuck on any of: option replication or hedging, delta, risk-neutral or martingale probabilities, binomial or lattice trees, backward induction, early exercise, or why an option price does… ([ricebusiness/plugins](https://github.com/ricebusiness/plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/tutor/skills/binomial))
- **binomial-co-occurrence-statistic-implementation**: Use when hunting for genes expressed almost exclusively in one cell cluster (bDEGs) in a scRNA-seq Seurat object, and when per-cluster marker scores must be normalized to [0,1] so they are comparable across clusters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-co-occurrence-statistic-implementation))
- **binomial-detection-probability-modeling**: Use when planning a single-cell RNA-seq experiment to determine the cell number N needed to detect a rare population (e.g., 5% prevalence) while accounting for within-population variability and capture efficiency. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-detection-probability-modeling))
- **binomial-distribution-based-tfbs-identification**: Use when predicting sequence or DNA shape motifs and TFBSs from trained DESSO CNN models on ENCODE ChIP-seq peaks and an objective, statistically grounded motif cutoff is needed instead of a manual threshold. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binomial-distribution-based-tfbs-identification))
- **binomial-enrichment-testing-with-bonferroni-correction**: Use when you have cell-attribute-aware clusters (Eva_partitions output over an igraph cell-cell nearest-neighbour graph) and need to flag which communities are significantly enriched for a condition label before downstream DoDA regression. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-enrichment-testing-with-bonferroni-correction))
- **binomial-fitting-of-fatty-acid-uptake-fraction**: Use when per-cell (or per-pixel) 13C6-glucose isotopologue distributions of fatty acids (e.g. C16 palmitate, C18 oleate) are available and the goal is to quantify relative uptake vs de novo synthesis as a fitted uptake fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binomial-fitting-of-fatty-acid-uptake-fraction))
- **binomial-hypothesis-testing**: Use when counting strand-annotated feature occurrences (same vs opposite orientation) yields success/failure counts and you need a P-value for deviation from p = 0.5, e.g., motif-in-region strand bias tests. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binomial-hypothesis-testing))
- **binomial-isotopologue-distribution-fitting**: Use when input consists of isotope-tracing (13C6-glucose) isotopologue distributions of fatty acids (e.g. palmitate, stearate, oleate) from MALDI-MSI or single-cell SpaceM data that are already corrected for natural isotope abundance and normalized, and you need p and uptake fractions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binomial-isotopologue-distribution-fitting))
- **binomial-labelling-model-inference**: Use when you have normalized, natural-isotope-corrected isotopologue distributions for a fatty acid (e.g. palmitate M+0..M+16) and need the glucose-derived acetyl-CoA labelling degree p per cell, sample, or pixel. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/binomial-labelling-model-inference))
- **binomial-mixture-model-variant-selection**: Use when single-cell mtDNA AD/DP matrices (from cellsnp-lite or cellSNP) are available and you must distinguish clone-discriminative heteroplasmic variants from noisy, non-inherited SNVs before clonal reconstruction with vireoSNP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-mixture-model-variant-selection))
- **binomial-mixture-variant-scoring**: Use when you have a SNP-by-cell AD/DP count matrix (e.g. from cellsnp-lite/cellSNP over mtDNA) and need to separate clone-discriminative heteroplasmic mtDNA variants from noisy background variants before clonal assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-mixture-variant-scoring))
- **binomial-model-snv-genotyping**: Use when calling SNVs from ONT long-read pileups and candidate loci have already been classified as high-quality (known 1000G variants with R_alt &gt; 0.3, or unknown variants with R_alt &gt; 0.7 and no neighbor within 10 bp), and a statistical genotype assignment is needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/binomial-model-snv-genotyping))
- **binomial-occupancy-probability-estimation**: Use when a raw OTU/ASV count table from an eDNA amplicon study includes PCR replicate metadata, and contamination or sporadic detection must be removed before taxonomic grouping and downstream community analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/binomial-occupancy-probability-estimation))
- **binomial-pricing**: Price American options and capture the early-exercise premium via a Cox-Ross-Rubinstein binomial tree. Use this skill whenever the user asks about American-style options, dividend-paying stocks with optimal exercise, early exercise decisions, or compares American vs European pricing. Triggers: "American option", "early exercise", "dividend exercise", "exercise premium", "CRR tree", "binomial", "lattice", "should I exercise this option early", "American put on TSLA", "is it ever optimal to exercise". Activate even with partial input — defaults: r = 4.3%, q = 0, steps = 200. ([dongzhuoyao/finance-option-skills](https://github.com/dongzhuoyao/finance-option-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/option-pricing/skills/binomial-pricing) · ★1)
- **binomial-probabilistic-alignment-scoring**: Use when long reads (PacBio/ONT FASTQ) already have candidate approximate mappings from a minimizer-based stage and you need per-location posterior mapping qualities plus binomial-approximation alignment identities before EM-based composition estimation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabarcoding/tree/HEAD/collections/metabarcoding/v1/leaves/binomial-probabilistic-alignment-scoring))
- **binomial-probability-threshold-modeling**: Use when computing minimizer-sharing thresholds for (w,k)-minimizer queries whose minimizer count varies per read (e.g. m in 15-35), so that random sharing can be separated from true matches at significance level 0.99. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-probability-threshold-modeling))
- **binomial-significance-testing**: Use when reproducing or extending DESSO's TFBS identification step, where motif instance candidates from a CNN motif detector must be statistically assessed with binomial P-values at varying signal cutoffs instead of a fixed subjective cutoff. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binomial-significance-testing))
- **binomial-strand-orientation-testing**: Use when you have BED files with '+'/'-' strand annotations for both motifs (e.g., TFBS occurrences) and regions (e.g., GENCODE transcribed regions) and need to quantify orientation bias per motif with statistical significance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binomial-strand-orientation-testing))
- **binomial-test-allele-count-analysis**: Use when you have a multi-sample VCF with raw allele depths per sample and need to classify biallelic SNPs as showing allele-specific expression versus equal (50/50) allelic balance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/binomial-test-allele-count-analysis))
- **binomial-test-snp-coverage-bias**: Use when allele-specific (haplotype-resolved) binding or looping from ChIA-PET data is needed and phased SNP genotypes (e.g., NA12878 hg19 Broad bundle) are available to assign haplotype labels to uniquely aligned reads. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/binomial-test-snp-coverage-bias))
- **binomial-test-with-bonferroni-correction**: Use when a strand-orientation count (e.g. template vs non-template or vs lagging occurrences of breakpoints or motifs) must be tested against a background orientation probability, with multiple element classes tested simultaneously.
